"""DEV: create and book a package through Angular and verify mobile form validation."""

import os, uuid, time, datetime, json
from smoke import login, request
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright, expect
from pathlib import Path

OUT = Path("docs/evidencias-integracion-fio")


def select_id(field, id):
    option = field.locator(f'option[value="{id}"],option[value$=": {id}"]')
    expect(option).to_have_count(1)
    field.select_option(value=option.get_attribute("value"))


def main():
    with sync_playwright() as p:
        browser = p.chromium.launch(
            headless=True,
            executable_path=os.environ.get("CHROMIUM_PATH", "/usr/bin/chromium"),
            args=["--no-sandbox"],
        )
        ac, ap, admin = login(browser, "admin-store", "Admin123!")
        cc, cp, client = login(browser, "cliente", "Cliente123!")
        sede = next(
            s for s in request("/sedes") if s["nombre"] == "Garritas Miraflores"
        )
        name = "Paquete de prueba " + uuid.uuid4().hex[:7]
        ap.get_by_role("button", name="Servicios por sede", exact=True).click()
        ap.get_by_label("Nombre de prestación", exact=True).fill(name)
        ap.get_by_label("Descripción de prestación", exact=True).fill(
            "Consulta y vacuna en un solo paquete"
        )
        ap.get_by_role("group", name="Componentes del paquete (opcional)").get_by_role(
            "checkbox", name="Consulta veterinaria", exact=True
        ).check()
        ap.get_by_role("group", name="Componentes del paquete (opcional)").get_by_role(
            "checkbox", name="Vacunación", exact=True
        ).check()
        with ap.expect_response(
            lambda r: r.url.endswith("/prestaciones")
            and r.request.method == "POST"
            and r.status == 201
        ):
            ap.get_by_role("button", name="Guardar prestación", exact=True).click()
        package = next(
            x for x in request("/prestaciones", admin) if x["nombre"] == name
        )
        select_id(ap.get_by_label("Sede de atención", exact=True), sede["id"])
        select_id(ap.get_by_label("Prestación para la sede", exact=True), package["id"])
        ap.get_by_label("Precio de la prestación", exact=True).fill("85")
        ap.get_by_label("Duración en minutos", exact=True).fill("60")
        with ap.expect_response(
            lambda r: r.url.endswith("/prestaciones")
            and r.request.method == "PUT"
            and r.status == 200
        ):
            ap.get_by_role("button", name="Guardar oferta", exact=True).click()
        expect(ap.get_by_role("heading", name=name, exact=True)).to_be_visible()
        ap.screenshot(path=str(OUT / "admin-paquetes.png"), full_page=True)
        cp.get_by_role("button", name="Servicios por sede", exact=True).click()
        select_id(cp.get_by_label("Sede de atención", exact=True), sede["id"])
        cp.get_by_role("button", name="Reservar " + name, exact=True).click()
        expect(
            cp.get_by_role("heading", name="Reservar cita", exact=True)
        ).to_be_visible()
        expect(cp.get_by_role("checkbox", name=name, exact=False)).to_be_checked()
        pet = request("/usuarios/me/mascotas", client)[0]
        pro = next(
            x
            for x in request("/especialistas")
            if x["activo"] and (x.get("sedeId") is None or x["sedeId"] == sede["id"])
        )
        select_id(cp.get_by_label("Mascota para la cita", exact=True), pet["id"])
        select_id(cp.get_by_label("Profesional", exact=True), pro["id"])
        ts = int(time.time() + 3600 * 24 * 500 + int(uuid.uuid4().hex[:3], 16) * 1800)
        ts -= ts % 1800
        cp.get_by_label("Fecha y hora de la cita", exact=True).fill(
            datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(ts, datetime.timezone.utc).strftime(
                "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M"
            )
        )
        cp.get_by_label("Motivo de la cita", exact=True).fill(name)
        with cp.expect_response(
            lambda r: r.url.endswith("/citas")
            and r.request.method == "POST"
            and r.status == 201
        ):
            cp.get_by_role("button", name="Reservar cita", exact=True).click()
        cita = next(x for x in request("/citas", client) if x["motivo"] == name)
        assert (
            cita["total"] == 85
            and "Consulta veterinaria" in cita["detalles"][0]["componentes"]
        )
        cp.screenshot(path=str(OUT / "cita-paquete.png"), full_page=True)
        # Validate the persisted profile form rejects a malformed mobile in the browser.
        cp.get_by_role("button", name="Mi cuenta y mascotas", exact=True).click()
        phone = cp.get_by_label("Teléfono de contacto", exact=True)
        phone.fill("12345")
        expect(
            cp.get_by_role("button", name="Guardar perfil", exact=True)
        ).to_be_disabled()
        phone.fill("987654321")
        expect(
            cp.get_by_role("button", name="Guardar perfil", exact=True)
        ).to_be_enabled()
        request("/citas/" + str(cita["id"]) + "/cancelar", client, "POST")
        request("/prestaciones/" + str(package["id"]), admin, "DELETE", expected=204)
        result = {
            "crear_paquete_desde_angular": True,
            "asignar_precio_y_sede_desde_angular": True,
            "reservar_paquete_desde_angular": cita["id"],
            "precio_y_componentes_guardados": True,
            "celular_invalido_bloquea_guardar": True,
        }
        (OUT / "frontend-paquetes.json").write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n")
        print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
        browser.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

resiliencia_logistica.py

uso-rapido/scripts/resiliencia_logistica.py

Archivo de soporte versionado. La ruta y el contenido completo permiten reconstruir la misma versión sin depender de archivos locales ocultos.

Crear: ejecuta este bloque desde la raíz. Después pega el siguiente contenido completo, guarda y continúa con la próxima ficha.

if (!(Test-Path $archivo)) { New-Item -ItemType File $archivo | Out-Null }
code $archivo

Contenido completo del archivo:

"""S6 DEV: suspend only the known local logistics process, verify bounded failure, recover.
Never run against another host or a production stack.
"""

import json, os, signal, time, uuid, subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright
from smoke import login, request, wait_state

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def main():
    with sync_playwright() as p:
        browser = p.chromium.launch(
            headless=True,
            executable_path=os.environ.get("CHROMIUM_PATH", "/usr/bin/chromium"),
            args=["--no-sandbox"],
        )
        ac, ap, admin = login(browser, "admin-store", "Admin123!")
        cc, cp, client = login(browser, "cliente", "Cliente123!")
        category = request(
            "/categorias",
            admin,
            "POST",
            {"nombre": "Resiliencia logística " + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]},
            expected=201,
        )
        product = request(
            "/productos",
            admin,
            "POST",
            {
                "nombre": "Prueba timeout " + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8],
                "precio": 10,
                "stock": 5,
                "activo": True,
                "categoriaId": category["id"],
            },
            expected=201,
        )
        coverage = next(c for c in request("/coberturas") if c["activo"])
        body = {
            "sedeId": request("/sedes")[0]["id"],
            "metodoPago": "YAPE",
            "detalles": [{"idProducto": product["id"], "cantidad": 1}],
            "tipoEntrega": "ENVIO",
            "zonaId": coverage["zonaId"],
            "transportistaId": coverage["transportistaId"],
            "tarifaCotizada": coverage["tarifa"],
            "direccionEntrega": "Av. Prueba 123, Lima",
        }
        pid = int((ROOT / ".runtime/garritas-envio-ms.pid").read_text())
        assert (
            b".runtime/garritas-envio-ms/app.jar"
            in Path(f"/proc/{pid}/cmdline").read_bytes()
        )
        times = []
        try:
            os.kill(pid, signal.SIGSTOP)
            for i in range(6):
                start = time.monotonic()
                request(
                    "/ventas", client, "POST", body, key=str(uuid.uuid4()), expected=503
                )
                times.append(round(time.monotonic() - start, 3))
            assert max(times) < 8, times
            assert times[-1] < 1.5, times
            assert not any(
                any(d["idProducto"] == product["id"] for d in v["detalles"])
                for v in request("/ventas", client)
            )
            assert request("/productos/" + str(product["id"]))["stock"] == 5
        finally:
            os.kill(pid, signal.SIGCONT)
        # The configured open state lasts ten seconds. Wait only long enough to probe recovery.
        time.sleep(11)
        sale = request(
            "/ventas", client, "POST", body, key=str(uuid.uuid4()), expected=201
        )
        wait_state(sale["id"], client, "PAGADA")
        request("/productos/" + str(product["id"]), admin, "DELETE", expected=204)
        result = {
            "fallos_controlados_503": 6,
            "segundos_por_peticion": times,
            "sin_venta_ni_cobro_durante_fallo": True,
            "circuito_abierto_ultima_peticion": times[-1] < 1.5,
            "recuperacion_pagada": sale["id"],
        }
        (ROOT / "docs/evidencias-integracion-fio/resiliencia.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n"
        )
        print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
        browser.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""DEV-only: prove Gateway balances Institution and survives graceful removal of a second instance."""
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,time,urllib.request,json
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

def get(url):
    with urllib.request.urlopen(url,timeout=4) as r:return r.read(),r.headers

def until(check,timeout=100):
    end=time.monotonic()+timeout
    last=None
    while time.monotonic()<end:
        try:
            result=check()
            if result:return result
        except Exception as e:last=e
        time.sleep(1)
    raise AssertionError('Tiempo de descubrimiento agotado: '+str(last))

def main():
    jar=ROOT/'.runtime/garritas-institucion-ms/app.jar'
    assert jar.exists(),'Inicia primero DEV con dev.sh all'
    try:get('http://localhost:18082/actuator/health/readiness')
    except OSError:pass
    else:raise AssertionError('El puerto de prueba 18082 ya está ocupado')
    logdir=ROOT/'.runtime/garritas-institucion-segunda';logdir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    env=os.environ.copy();env['SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE']='dev';env['LOG_PATH']=str(logdir);env['JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS']='-Xms64m -Xmx384m';env.pop('LOGGING_CONFIG',None)
    java=str(Path(env['JAVA_HOME'])/'bin/java') if env.get('JAVA_HOME') else 'java'
    with (logdir/'console.log').open('ab') as log:
        process=subprocess.Popen([java,'-jar',str(jar),'--server.port=18082'],cwd=ROOT/'services/garritas-institucion-ms',env=env,stdout=log,stderr=log,stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        try:
            until(lambda:get('http://localhost:18082/actuator/health/readiness'))
            seen=set()
            def balanced():
                _,headers=get('http://localhost:18080/api/v1/sedes');instance=headers.get('X-Service-Instance');seen.add(instance)
                return {'garritas-institucion-ms:8082','garritas-institucion-ms:18082'}<=seen
            until(balanced)
            print('PASS: dos instancias en Eureka; Gateway alterna X-Service-Instance 8082 y 18082',flush=True)
        finally:
            process.terminate()
            try:process.wait(timeout=15)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:process.kill();process.wait()
    # Wait for deregistration to propagate before asserting a stable remaining set.
    def remaining():
        headers=[get('http://localhost:18080/api/v1/sedes')[1].get('X-Service-Instance') for _ in range(6)]
        return all(h=='garritas-institucion-ms:8082' for h in headers)
    until(remaining)
    print('PASS: instancia secundaria detenida; Gateway continúa sirviendo la instancia principal',flush=True)

if __name__=='__main__':main()

"""Real PKCE browser + Gateway + PostgreSQL + Kafka integration checks.
Requires DEV stack, Angular at :4200, and playwright (Chromium or CHROMIUM_PATH).
Fixture products are deactivated; categories and sale/payment audit history are retained.
"""
import json
import os
import time
import uuid
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright, expect

API = os.environ.get('API_URL', 'http://localhost:18080/api/v1')
WEB = os.environ.get('WEB_URL', 'http://localhost:4200')


def request(path, token=None, method='GET', body=None, key=None, expected=200):
    headers = {'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Trace-ID': 'smoke-garritas'}
    if token:
        headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ' + token
    if key:
        headers['Idempotency-Key'] = key
    req = urllib.request.Request(API + path, data=None if body is None else json.dumps(body).encode(), headers=headers, method=method)
    try:
        response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
        response = error
    data = response.read()
    assert response.status == expected, f'{method} {path}: expected {expected}, got {response.status}: {data[:250]!r}'
    return json.loads(data) if data else None


def sign_in(page, username, password, button='Iniciar sesión'):
    page.get_by_role('button', name=button, exact=True).click()
    page.locator('#username').fill(username)
    page.locator('#password').fill(password)
    with page.expect_response(lambda response: '/protocol/openid-connect/token' in response.url and response.status == 200) as exchange:
        page.locator('#kc-login').click()
    session = exchange.value.json()
    expect(page.get_by_role('button', name='Salir', exact=True)).to_be_visible(timeout=30000)
    assert session.get('access_token'), 'PKCE did not return an access token'
    assert page.evaluate('Object.values(localStorage).every(v=>!v.includes("access_token")&&!v.startsWith("eyJ"))')
    return session['access_token']


def login(browser, username, password):
    context = browser.new_context()
    page = context.new_page()
    page.goto(WEB)
    token = sign_in(page, username, password)
    return context, page, token


def wait_state(venta_id, token, wanted):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 45
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        venta = request('/ventas/' + str(venta_id), token)
        if venta['estado'] == wanted:
            return venta
        time.sleep(.5)
    raise AssertionError(f'Venta {venta_id}: expected {wanted}, got {venta["estado"]}')


def main():
    with sync_playwright() as p:
        executable = os.environ.get('CHROMIUM_PATH')
        browser = p.chromium.launch(headless=True, args=['--no-sandbox'], **({'executable_path': executable} if executable else {}))
        admin_context, _, admin = login(browser, 'admin-store', 'Admin123!')
        context = browser.new_context()
        page = context.new_page()
        suffix = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
        categoria = request('/categorias', admin, 'POST', {'nombre': 'Smoke ' + suffix, 'descripcion': 'Prueba de integración'}, expected=201)
        producto = request('/productos', admin, 'POST', {'nombre': 'Alimento ' + suffix, 'descripcion': 'Prueba de integración', 'precio': 12.5, 'stock': 10, 'activo': True, 'categoriaId': categoria['id']}, expected=201)
        toy = request('/productos', admin, 'POST', {'nombre': 'Juguete ' + suffix, 'descripcion': 'Prueba de carrito', 'precio': 7.99, 'stock': 5, 'activo': True, 'categoriaId': categoria['id']}, expected=201)
        try:
            page.goto(WEB)
            page.get_by_role('button', name='Nueva orden', exact=True).click()
            page.get_by_role('combobox', name='Sede de la compra', exact=True).select_option(label='Garritas Miraflores · Lima')
            expect(page.get_by_role('heading', name='Nueva orden', exact=True)).to_be_visible()
            page.get_by_role('combobox', name='Categoría', exact=True).select_option(label='Alimentos')
            expect(page.get_by_role('button', name='Añadir Alimento para gato adulto 1 kg al carrito', exact=True)).to_be_visible()
            page.get_by_role('combobox', name='Categoría', exact=True).select_option(label='Todas las categorías')
            page.get_by_role('searchbox', name='Buscar productos').fill('gato adulto')
            expect(page.locator('.product-card')).to_have_count(1)
            page.get_by_role('searchbox', name='Buscar productos').fill('')
            page.get_by_role('button', name='Añadir ' + producto['nombre'] + ' al carrito', exact=True).click()
            page.get_by_role('button', name='Añadir ' + toy['nombre'] + ' al carrito', exact=True).click()
            quantity = page.get_by_label('Cantidad de ' + producto['nombre'], exact=True)
            quantity.fill('2'); quantity.press('Tab')
            expect(page.get_by_test_id('cart-total')).to_contain_text('32.99')
            page.get_by_role('button', name='Quitar ' + toy['nombre'], exact=True).click()
            expect(page.get_by_test_id('cart-total')).to_contain_text('25.00')
            quantity.fill('1'); quantity.press('Tab')
            expect(page.get_by_test_id('cart-total')).to_contain_text('12.50')
            cliente = sign_in(page, 'cliente', 'Cliente123!', 'Inicia sesión para comprar')
            expect(page.get_by_label('Cantidad de ' + producto['nombre'], exact=True)).to_have_value('1')
            page.reload()
            expect(page.get_by_role('button', name='Salir', exact=True)).to_be_visible(timeout=30000)
            expect(page.get_by_label('Cantidad de ' + producto['nombre'], exact=True)).to_have_value('1')
            print('PASS: Nueva orden, catálogo de mascotas, filtros, cantidades, subtotales y quitar productos')
            print('PASS: carrito conservado al iniciar sesión y recargar, sesión PKCE sin tokens persistidos')
            request('/ventas', expected=401)
            request('/productos', cliente, 'POST', {}, expected=403)
            request('/productos/' + str(producto['id']))
            print('PASS: catálogo público, ventas protegidas y escritura solo ADMIN')
            with page.expect_response(lambda r: r.request.method=='POST' and r.url.endswith('/api/v1/ventas')) as created:
                page.get_by_role('button', name='Confirmar compra', exact=True).click()
            assert created.value.status==201
            current=next(v for v in request('/ventas',cliente) if any(d['idProducto']==producto['id'] for d in v['detalles']))
            wait_state(current['id'],cliente,'PAGADA')
            expect(page.get_by_role('heading', name='Mis pedidos', exact=True)).to_be_visible()
            current_row=page.locator('article.order').filter(has=page.get_by_role('heading',name='Pedido #'+str(current['id']),exact=True))
            expect(current_row.locator('.badge')).to_have_text('Pagada', timeout=45000)
            expect(page.get_by_role('button', name='Carrito, 0 unidades', exact=True)).to_be_visible()
            assert request('/productos/' + str(producto['id']))['stock'] == 9
            print('PASS: compra desde Angular, CORS, reserva, pago Kafka y polling hasta PAGADA')
            body = {'sedeId': request('/sedes')[0]['id'], 'metodoPago': 'TRANSFERENCIA', 'detalles': [{'idProducto': producto['id'], 'cantidad': 2}]}
            key = str(uuid.uuid4())
            venta = request('/ventas', cliente, 'POST', body, key, expected=201)
            assert request('/ventas', cliente, 'POST', body, key, expected=201)['id'] == venta['id']
            modified = {**body, 'metodoPago': 'YAPE'}
            request('/ventas', cliente, 'POST', modified, key, expected=409)
            wait_state(venta['id'], cliente, 'CANCELADA')
            assert request('/productos/' + str(producto['id']))['stock'] == 9
            assert request('/pagos/venta/' + str(venta['id']), cliente)['estado'] == 'RECHAZADO'
            print('PASS: rechazo, compensación de stock e idempotencia HTTP con conflicto de payload')
            request('/ventas', cliente, 'POST', {'sedeId': request('/sedes')[0]['id'], 'metodoPago': 'YAPE', 'detalles': [{'idProducto': producto['id'], 'cantidad': 999}]}, str(uuid.uuid4()), expected=409)
            request('/ventas', cliente, 'POST', {'sedeId': request('/sedes')[0]['id'], 'metodoPago': 'YAPE', 'detalles': [{'idProducto': producto['id'], 'cantidad': 0}]}, str(uuid.uuid4()), expected=400)
            request('/ventas', cliente, 'POST', body, expected=400)
            print('PASS: sin sobreventa, cantidad inválida y clave obligatoria')
            preflight = urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(API + '/ventas', method='OPTIONS', headers={'Origin': WEB, 'Access-Control-Request-Method': 'POST', 'Access-Control-Request-Headers': 'authorization,content-type,idempotency-key'}))
            assert preflight.headers['Access-Control-Allow-Origin'] == WEB
            print('PASS: preflight CORS con Authorization e Idempotency-Key')
        finally:
            request('/productos/' + str(producto['id']), admin, 'DELETE', expected=204)
            request('/productos/' + str(toy['id']), admin, 'DELETE', expected=204)
            # Category remains associated with the deactivated product for audit.
            context.close()
            admin_context.close()
            browser.close()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

ETAPA 18 · Automatización y entrega

status.py

uso-rapido/scripts/status.py

Archivo de soporte versionado. La ruta y el contenido completo permiten reconstruir la misma versión sin depender de archivos locales ocultos.

Crear: ejecuta este bloque desde la raíz. Después pega el siguiente contenido completo, guarda y continúa con la próxima ficha.

$archivo = 'uso-rapido/scripts/status.py'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force (Split-Path $archivo) | Out-Null
if (!(Test-Path $archivo)) { New-Item -ItemType File $archivo | Out-Null }
code $archivo

Contenido completo del archivo:

"""Check useful application responses, including Kafka's actual cluster connection."""
import concurrent.futures
import json
import subprocess
import sys
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
TARGETS = [
    ('Tienda Angular', 4200, '/'),
    ('Kafka UI', 18085, '/api/clusters'),
    ('Keycloak', 18180, '/realms/garritas/.well-known/openid-configuration'),
    ('Eureka', 18761, '/actuator/health/readiness'),
    ('Config Server', 18888, '/actuator/health/readiness'),
    ('Gateway', 18080, '/actuator/health/readiness'),
    ('Catálogo por Gateway', 18080, '/api/v1/productos'),
    *[(name, port, '/actuator/health/readiness') for name, port in [
        ('Bienestar', 8088), ('Venta', 8081), ('Institución', 8082), ('Envío', 8083), ('Citas', 8084),
        ('Catálogo', 8085), ('Usuario', 8086), ('Pago', 8087)]],
]
if '--observability' in sys.argv:
    TARGETS += [('Grafana', 13000, '/api/health'), ('Prometheus', 19090, '/-/ready'), ('Loki', 13100, '/ready')]


def check(target):
    name, port, path = target
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(f'http://localhost:{port}{path}', timeout=5) as response:
            body = response.read()
            if name == 'Kafka UI':
                clusters = json.loads(body)
                assert clusters and all(str(c.get('status')).upper() == 'ONLINE' for c in clusters), 'Kafka no está conectado'
            elif name == 'Catálogo por Gateway':
                assert isinstance(json.loads(body), list)
            elif name == 'Keycloak':
                assert '/realms/garritas' in json.loads(body)['issuer']
            elif name == 'Tienda Angular':
                assert b'<app-root' in body
        return name, port, 'OK', True
    except Exception as error:
        return name, port, str(error), False


def main():
    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=5) as pool:
        results = list(pool.map(check, TARGETS))
    for name, port, detail, ok in results:
        print(f'{"OK" if ok else "FALLO":5} {name:24} puerto {port:<5} {"" if ok else detail}')
    broker = subprocess.run(['docker', 'compose', '-f', 'kafka/compose-dev.yml', 'exec', '-T', 'kafka', '/opt/kafka/bin/kafka-topics.sh', '--bootstrap-server', 'localhost:9092', '--list'], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)
    topics_ok = broker.returncode == 0 and 'garritas.ventas.v1' in broker.stdout
    print(f'{"OK" if topics_ok else "FALLO":5} Kafka broker y topic de ventas')
    return 0 if all(ok for *_, ok in results) and topics_ok else 1


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
